sinA sinB with A=2πx, B=2π(t-x). t=0.95, A over 0..1.9π. Let's use identity: sinA sinB = (1/2)[cos(A-B)-cos(A+B)]. A-B=2πx -2π(t-x)=4πx -2πt. A+B=2πt. Integral I = 1/2 [∫cos(4πx-2πt) dx - ∫cos2πt dx].
∫cos(4πx-2πt) dx = [sin(4πx-2πt)/(4π)]_0^t = (sin(4πt-2πt) - sin(-2πt))/(4π) = (sin2πt + sin2πt)/(4π)= sin2πt/(2π).
Times 1/2 gives sin2πt/(4π). ∫cos2πt dx = t cos2πt. Times -1/2 = -t/2 cos2πt. So I = sin2πt/(4π) - t/2 cos2πt. That's what we had.
```python
import numpy as np

from scipy.optimize import minimize, Nelder-Mead? (method='Nelder-Mead')

def _fft_len(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def conv_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size ==0: return np.inf
    n = a.size
    M = 1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()? Need 1 << (2*n-1 -1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    C = np.fft.irfft(A*A, m)[:2*n-1]
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12: return np.inf
    return 2.0*n*float(np.max(C))/(S*S)
```

Let's design `trig_seq(n,p,q)`:
```
x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
th = (2.0*np.pi/n)*x
a = 1.0 + p*np.cos(th) + q*np.sin(th)
np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
return a
```

Implement `fast_conv` carefully:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0: return np.array([])
    m = 2*n - 1
    M = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)[:m]
    return c
```
For small n, M power of 2. For n=2048, M=4096. good.

`score_and_conv`:
```
def score_and_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0: return np.inf, np.array([])
    n=a.size
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12: return np.inf, np.zeros(2*n-1)
    c=fast_conv(a)
    # FFT may have tiny negative, but max positive
    cb=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cb): return np.inf,c
    sc=2.0*n*cb/(S*S)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return np.inf,c
    return sc,c
```
We can use `np.maximum(c,0)` for max? Conv of nonneg should nonneg except FFT errors tiny negative; max likely positive. Use float(np.max(c)). If all zero return inf.

Mix details:
```
def mix_sequences(a,b,w):
   sa=a.sum(); sb=b.sum()
   if sa<1e-12 or sb<1e-12: return None
   return (1-w)*a/sa + w*b/sb
```

```
def peak_shave_polish(a_init, time_limit_s, t_start, seed=123):
    a = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size==0 or time_limit_s<=0: return np.inf,a
    a = np.maximum(a,0.0)
    if a.sum() <=0: return np.inf,a
    a = a / a.sum()
    best_s, cbest = score_and_conv(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_s): return np.inf,a
    best_a = a.copy()
    deadline = t_start + time_limit_s
    factors = (0.95,0.9,0.8,0.7,0.5)
    top_masks? 
    it=0
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and no_improve < 200:
        it+=1
        s, c = score_and_conv(a)
        if s < best_s:
            best_s=s; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve +=1
            if no_improve>200: break
        # top lags: take top 3 coefficients by value near max? The sequence has triangular shape, top near peak plus symmetry? linear conv not symmetric. Use top 3 by c.
        # Avoid including endpoints? endpoints large? c[0]=a0^2 maybe small. Top 3 okay.
        # Find active mask for top_t lags
        active=np.zeros(a.size, dtype=bool)
        # top indices descending but exclude duplicates? yes.
        vals = c.copy()
        for _ in range(3):
            idx = int(np.argmax(vals)); vals[idx]=-1e300
            k=idx
            L = max(0, k - a.size + 1)
            R = min(k, a.size - 1)
            active[L:R+1]=True
        if active.all():
            # if all active, try scaling no change, break
            factors??
        comp = ~active
        comp_count = int(comp.sum())
        if comp_count == 0:
            # no complement; maybe try random local perturb? skip
            continue
        comp_sum = float(a[comp].sum())
        act_sum = float(a[active].sum())
        if act_sum <= 0: continue
        best_cand = None
        for f in factors:
            a_test = a.copy()
            a_test[active] *= f
            removed = act_sum*(1-f)
            if removed > 0:
                if comp_sum > 1e-15:
                    a_test[comp] += removed * a[comp] / comp_sum
                else:
                    a_test[comp] += removed / comp_count
            a_test = np.maximum(a_test,0.0)
            # normalize due to numerical
            ss = a_test.sum()
            if ss <= 0: continue
            a_test /= ss
            sc,_=score_and_conv(a_test)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < s - 1e-12:
                a = a_test
                no_improve=0
                if sc < best_s: best_s=sc; best_a=a.copy()
                break
        # if no factor improved, maybe try increasing some complement? not.
    return best_s,best_a
```

```
def evaluate_trig_params(p,q,n,cos_arr,sin_arr,base):
    a = base + p*cos_arr + q*sin_arr
    if a.size:
       np.maximum(a,0.0,out=a)
```

Parent resample: `np.interp` expects x_new sorted, x_old arange len. If parent length 1 and new_n? handle.
```
def resample_sequence(a,new_n):
    n=a.size
    if n==new_n: return a.copy()
    if n==1: return np.full(new_n,float(a[0]))
    x_old=np.arange(n); x_new=np.linspace(0,n-1,new_n)
    y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    return np.maximum(y,0.0)
```

Mix:
```
def mix_sequences(a,b,w):
   if a is None or b is None: return None
   sa=a.sum(); sb=b.sum()
   if sa<1e-12 or sb<1e-12: return None
   out=(1-w)*a/sa + w*b/sb
   return np.maximum(out,0.0)
```

Direct refine loops:
```
def refine_trig_direct(p0,q0,n,span,steps,deadline,t_start):
    idx=np.arange(n); th=2π idx/n; cos_arr=np.cos(th); sin_arr=np.sin(th); base=np.ones(n)
    best_s=evaluate_trig_param(p0,q0,...)
    best_p=p0; best_q=q0
    ds=span/(2*steps)
    i=0
    stop=False
    while i<=2*steps and not stop:
      p=p0 + (i-steps)*ds
      j=0
      while j<=2*steps and not stop:
        q=q0 + (j-steps)*ds
        # optional limit norm to 1.2
        r=np.hypot(p,q)
        if r > 1.2:
          p=p*1.2/r; q=q*1.2/r
        if p != p0 or q != q0:
           s=evaluate_trig_param(...)
           if s<best_s: ...
        j+=1
        if time.monotonic()>deadline-5: stop=True
      i+=1
    return best_s,best_p,best_q
```

```
def peak_shave_polish(a_init, time_limit_s, t_start):
    a = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n=a.size
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s0=float(a.sum())
    if s0 <=1e-12 or n<4: return np.inf, a
    a=a/s0
    best_s, c = score_and_conv(a)
    best_a=a.copy()
    deadline = t_start + time_limit_s
    factors=(0.95,0.9,0.8,0.7,0.5)
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and no_improve < 120:
        it += 1
        s, c = score_and_conv(a)
        if not np.isfinite(s): break
        if s < best_s - 1e-12:
            best_s=s; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
        # top_count pattern
        top_count = (1,2,3,5)[(it-1)%4]
        active = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
        vals = c.copy()
        for _ in range(top_count):
            k = int(np.argmax(vals))
            vals[k] = -np.inf
            L = k - n + 1
            if L < 0: L = 0
            R = k
            if R >= n: R = n-1
            if R >= L:
                active[L:R+1] = True
        if not active.any():
            no_improve += 1
            continue
        comp = ~active
        comp_count = int(comp.sum())
        if comp_count ==0:
            no_improve += 1
            continue
        act_sum = float(a[active].sum())
        if act_sum <= 1e-15:
            no_improve += 1
            continue
        comp_sum = float(a[comp].sum())
        improved=False
        for f in factors:
            a_test = a.copy()
            a_test[active] = a[active] * f
            removed = act_sum * (1.0 - f)
            if removed > 0:
               if comp_sum > 1e-15:
                  # If comp_sum small but > eps, proportional might be huge? fine.
                  a_test[comp] += removed * a[comp] / comp_sum
               else:
                  a_test[comp] += removed / comp_count
            np.maximum(a_test,0.0,out=a_test)
            sc,_=score_and_conv(a_test)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < s - 1e-12:
                a = a_test
                # normalize for stability if sum changed
                ss = float(a.sum())
                if ss > 1e-12:
                   a = a / ss
                best_s = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                no_improve=0
                improved=True
                break
        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
    return best_s,best_a
```
One bug: We compute s,c at loop start, then if factor improves we set a to a_test scored sc; then next loop recomputes. Good. If no factor, no_improve. If best_s updated at start due to previous accepted? start score equals best_s typically; no issue.

Could `vals[k] = -np.inf` and then next argmax if all -inf? top_count <=5, length >, okay.

```
def evaluate_trig_param(p, q, base, cos_arr, sin_arr):
    a = base + p * cos_arr + q * sin_arr
    np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
    s, _ = score_and_conv(a)
    return s
```

```
def make_trig_seq(n,p,q):
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    th=2*np.pi*x/float(n)
    a=1.0 + p*np.cos(th) + q*np.sin(th)
    np.maximum(a,0.0,out=a)
    return a
```

```
def trig_coarse_search(n, deadline):
    twopi=2*np.pi
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64); th=twopi*x/n
    cos_arr=np.cos(th); sin_arr=np.sin(th); base=np.ones(n)
    best_p=0.394; best_q=-0.919
    best_s=evaluate_trig_param(best_p,best_q,base,cos_arr,sin_arr)
    r_vals=(0.5,0.7,0.8,0.9,0.95,1.0,1.05,1.1,1.15)
    u_vals=np.linspace(-0.5,0.5,201)
    stop=False
    for r in r_vals:
       if stop: break
       for u in u_vals:
          p=r*np.cos(twopi*u); q=r*np.sin(twopi*u)
          s=evaluate_trig_param(p,q,base,cos_arr,sin_arr)
          if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
             best_s=s; best_p=p; best_q=q
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20:
             stop=True; break
    return best_p,best_q,best_s
```
If n small and base sum? ok.

`refine_trig_direct(p0,q0,n,span,steps,deadline)`:
```
def refine_trig_direct(p0,q0,n,span,steps,deadline):
    twopi=2*np.pi
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64); th=twopi*x/n; cos_arr=...; sin_arr=...; base=np.ones(n)
    best_p=p0; best_q=q0; best_s=evaluate_trig_param(...)
    ds = span/(2.0*steps) if steps>0 else span
    stop = False
    i=0
    while i <= 2*steps and not stop:
       p = p0 + (i-steps)*ds
       j=0
       while j <= 2*steps and not stop:
          q = q0 + (j-steps)*ds
          r = np.hypot(p,q)
          if r > 1.2:
             p = p*(1.2/r); q = q*(1.2/r)
          if (abs(p-p0)>1e-14 or abs(q-q0)>1e-14):
             s=evaluate_trig_param(p,q,base,cos_arr,sin_arr)
             if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
                best_s=s; best_p=p; best_q=q
          j += 1
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10:
             stop=True
       i += 1
    return best_p,best_q,best_s
```
The center evaluated before. If r>1.2 scaling may move to boundary; okay. If p0/q0 norm>1.2? from coarse max 1.15. Good.

```python
"""
Searches for a low-score non-negative step-height sequence.

Main idea:
  - The objective is scale invariant.  A strong global attractor is a
    non-negative one-harmonic profile
        a_i = max(0, 1 + p*cos(2*pi*i/n) + q*sin(2*pi*i/n)).
  - We grid-search / locally refine (p, q) at several FFT-backed discrete
    resolutions, compare with resampled parent sequences and mixes, then apply
    a safe peak-shaving coordinate heuristic that only accepts improvements.
  - The best sequence is normalized to sum 1 before returning.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def fast_conv(a):
    """Linear convolution a * a via real FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if m <= 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]] if n == 1 else [0.0])
    M = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)
    return c[:m]


def score_and_conv(a):
    """
    Returns (score, convolution).
    score = 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2, matching evaluate_sequence
    for valid non-negative finite input.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.array([])
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return np.inf, np.array([0.0] * (2 * n - 1))

    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf, np.array([0.0] * (2 * n - 1))

    c = fast_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf, c

    # FFT can create tiny negative values; max should still be positive for
    # non-zero non-negative inputs.
    max_c = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(max_c) or max_c <= 0.0:
        return np.inf, c

    s = 2.0 * float(n) * max_c / (S * S)
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf, c
    return s, c


def score_sequence(a):
    """Score only."""
    return score_and_conv(a)[0]


def make_trig_seq(n, p, q):
    """Generate a non-negative sinusoidal profile of length n."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([])
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    th = (2.0 * np.pi / float(n)) * x
    a = 1.0 + p * np.cos(th) + q * np.sin(th)
    np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
    return a


def evaluate_trig_param(p, q, base, cos_arr, sin_arr):
    """Score a trig candidate using precomputed cosine/sine arrays."""
    a = base + p * cos_arr + q * sin_arr
    np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
    s, _ = score_and_conv(a)
    return s


def trig_coarse_search(n, deadline):
    """
    Coarse polar grid search over one-harmonic profiles at fixed n.
    Returns (p, q, score).
    """
    n = int(n)
    twopi = 2.0 * np.pi
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    th = (twopi / float(n)) * x
    cos_arr = np.cos(th)
    sin_arr = np.sin(th)
    base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    # Strong empirical/continuous starting point.
    best_p = 0.394
    best_q = -0.919
    best_s = evaluate_trig_param(best_p, best_q, base, cos_arr, sin_arr)
    if not np.isfinite(best_s):
        best_s = np.inf

    r_vals = (0.5, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9, 0.95, 1.0, 1.05, 1.1, 1.15)
    u_vals = np.linspace(-0.5, 0.5, 201)

    stop = False
    for r in r_vals:
        if stop:
            break
        for u in u_vals:
            p = r * np.cos(twopi * u)
            q = r * np.sin(twopi * u)
            s = evaluate_trig_param(p, q, base, cos_arr, sin_arr)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
                best_s = s
                best_p = float(p)
                best_q = float(q)
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
                stop = True
                break

    return best_p, best_q, best_s


def refine_trig_direct(p0, q0, n, span, steps, deadline):
    """
    Refine (p, q) by a direct small grid around a point, at fixed n.
    Keeps norm <= 1.2; values outside the ideal non-negative radius are
    clipped in score evaluation anyway.
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0 or span <= 0.0 or steps <= 0:
        return p0, q0, np.inf

    twopi = 2.0 * np.pi
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    th = (twopi / float(n)) * x
    cos_arr = np.cos(th)
    sin_arr = np.sin(th)
    base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    best_p = float(p0)
    best_q = float(q0)
    best_s = evaluate_trig_param(best_p, best_q, base, cos_arr, sin_arr)
    if not np.isfinite(best_s):
        best_s = np.inf

    ds = span / (2.0 * float(steps))
    stop = False
    i = 0
    while i <= 2 * steps and not stop:
        p = best_p if False else p0 + (i - steps) * ds
        j = 0
        while j <= 2 * steps and not stop:
            q = q0 + (j - steps) * ds

            norm_pq = float(np.hypot(p, q))
            if norm_pq > 1.2:
                scale = 1.2 / max(norm_pq, 1e-12)
                p *= scale
                q *= scale

            if abs(p - p0) > 1e-14 or abs(q - q0) > 1e-14:
                s = evaluate_trig_param(p, q, base, cos_arr, sin_arr)
                if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
                    best_s = s
                    best_p = float(p)
                    best_q = float(q)

            j += 1
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
                stop = True
        i += 1

    return best_p, best_q, best_s


def resample_sequence(a, new_n):
    """Linear resampling to a new length."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.array([])
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(new_n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)

    x_old = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, float(n - 1), new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    np.maximum(y, 0.0, out=y)
    return y


def mix_sequences(a, b, w):
    """Mix two non-negative sequences after normalizing both to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0 or b.size == 0 or a.size != b.size:
        return None
    sa = float(a.sum())
    sb = float(b.sum())
    if sa <= 1e-12 or sb <= 1e-12:
        return None
    out = (1.0 - float(w)) * (a / sa) + float(w) * (b / sb)
    np.maximum(out, 0.0, out=out)
    return out


def peak_shave_polish(a_init, time_limit_s, t_start):
    """
    Safe heuristic local polish:
      - identify top convolution lags,
      - shrink the indices that participate in those lags,
      - redistribute the removed mass to complement variables,
      - accept only exact-score improvements.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = a.size
    if n < 8 or time_limit_s <= 0.0:
        return np.inf, a

    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S0 = float(a.sum())
    if S0 <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf, a
    a = a / S0

    best_s, c = score_and_conv(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_s):
        return np.inf, a
    best_a = a.copy()

    deadline = t_start + float(time_limit_s)
    factors = (0.95, 0.90, 0.80, 0.70, 0.50)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and no_improve < 120:
        it += 1
        s, c = score_and_conv(a)
        if not np.isfinite(s):
            break

        if s < best_s - 1e-12:
            best_s = s
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0

        top_count = (1, 2, 3, 5)[(it - 1) % 4]
        active = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
        vals = c.copy()

        for _ in range(top_count):
            k = int(np.argmax(vals))
            vals[k] = -np.inf
            L = k - n + 1
            R = k
            if L < 0:
                L = 0
            if R >= n:
                R = n - 1
            if R >= L:
                active[L:R + 1] = True

        if not active.any():
            no_improve += 1
            continue

        comp = ~active
        comp_count = int(comp.sum())
        if comp_count <= 0:
            no_improve += 1
            continue

        act_sum = float(a[active].sum())
        if act_sum <= 1e-15:
            no_improve += 1
            continue

        comp_sum = float(a[comp].sum())
        improved = False

        for f in factors:
            a_test = a.copy()
            a_test[active] = a[active] * f
            removed = act_sum * (1.0 - f)

            if removed > 0.0:
                if comp_sum > 1e-15:
                    a_test[comp] += removed * a[comp] / comp_sum
                else:
                    a_test[comp] += removed / float(comp_count)

            np.maximum(a_test, 0.0, out=a_test)

            sc, _ = score_and_conv(a_test)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < s - 1e-12:
                a = a_test
                ss = float(a.sum())
                if ss > 1e-12:
                    a = a / ss
                best_s = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1

    return best_s, best_a


def consider_candidate(a, best_s, best_a, label):
    """Update best if a candidate is strictly better.  Prints when it updates."""
    if a is None:
        return best_s, best_a
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_s, best_a
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(a.sum()) <= 1e-12:
        return best_s, best_a

    s, _ = score_and_conv(a)
    if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
        print(f"NEW BEST {s:.8f} | {label} | n={a.size}")
        return s, a.copy()
    return best_s, best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Returns the best non-negative height sequence found within the budget.
    """
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2400.0

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + max(60.0, budget - 30.0)

    best_s = np.inf
    best_a = None
    best_p = 0.394
    best_q = -0.919

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Parent loaded by the harness, if available.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    parent = None
    try:
        hs1 = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if hs1 is not None:
            parent = np.asarray(hs1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if parent.size < 8:
                parent = None
            else:
                parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if float(parent.sum()) <= 1e-12:
                    parent = None
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        best_s, best_a = consider_candidate(parent, best_s, best_a, "parent original")
        print(f"Parent initial best: {best_s:.8f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Global one-harmonic search.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
        p, q, sc = trig_coarse_search(2048, deadline)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            best_p, best_q = p, q
            print(f"Trig coarse n=2048: p={p:.6f}, q={q:.6f}, score={sc:.8f}")

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
            p, q, sc = refine_trig_direct(best_p, best_q, 4096, 0.12, 20, deadline)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                best_p, best_q = p, q
            print(f"Trig refine n=4096: p={p:.6f}, q={q:.6f}, score={sc:.8f}")

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0:
            p, q, sc = refine_trig_direct(best_p, best_q, 16384, 0.04, 10, deadline)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                best_p, best_q = p, q
            print(f"Trig refine n=16384: p={p:.6f}, q={q:.6f}, score={sc:.8f}")

        n_options = (2048, 4096, 8192, 16384)
        for n in n_options:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            a = make_trig_seq(n, best_p, best_q)
            best_s, best_a = consider_candidate(a, best_s, best_a, f"trig n={n}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Parent resampling and mixing with the best one-harmonic profile.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if parent is not None:
        n_options = (2048, 4096, 8192, 16384)
        for n in n_options:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
                break
            a_par = resample_sequence(parent, n)
            best_s, best_a = consider_candidate(a_par, best_s, best_a, f"parent resample n={n}")

        mix_weights = (0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9)
        for n in n_options:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
                break
            a_trig = make_trig_seq(n, best_p, best_q)
            a_par = resample_sequence(parent, n)
            for w in mix_weights:
                mx = mix_sequences(a_trig, a_par, w)
                best_s, best_a = consider_candidate(mx, best_s, best_a, f"mix trig+parent w={w} n={n}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Fallback if everything failed.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_s):
        fallback = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64)
        best_s, best_a = score_and_conv(fallback)
        print(f"Fallback uniform score: {best_s:.8f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Safe local peak-shaving polish on the current best.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if time_left > 45.0 and best_a is not None:
        polish_limit = min(300.0, max(20.0, time_left - 15.0))
        print(f"Starting peak-shave polish with limit {polish_limit:.0f}s")
        ps, pa = peak_shave_polish(best_a, polish_limit, time.monotonic())
        if np.isfinite(ps) and ps < best_s:
            best_s = ps
            best_a = pa.copy()
            print(f"Polish improved best to {best_s:.8f}")
        else:
            print(f"Polish stopped. Best remains {best_s:.8f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final validation / normalization.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    out_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    out_a = np.clip(out_a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out_sum = float(out_a.sum())
    if out_sum < 0.01:
        out_a = np.full(64, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
        out_sum = float(out_a.sum())

    # Scale to sum 1.  The objective is scale-invariant; this keeps final
    # values moderate and ensures sum > 0.01.
    out_a = out_a / out_sum
    out = [float(x) for x in out_a]

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = np.inf

    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal score = {best_s:.8f}")
    print(f"FINAL verified score = {verified:.8f}")
    print(f"FINAL length = {len(out)}")
    print(f"FINAL elapsed = {time.monotonic() - t_start:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 70)

    return out
```
